*At Matins: Canon of the saints on 6; after Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the saints; after Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 8:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Fathers of the Ecumenical Councils, Tone 8:*  
The proclamation of the apostles and the teachings of the fathers have sealed the one faith of the Church, which, wearing the robe of truth woven of theology from above, rightly divides and glorifies the great mystery of piety!  
*Exapostilarion of the saints; Both now, Theotokion. At the Praises: 3 prosomoia of the Holy Fathers, on 6, Tone 6, Podoben: "Having set aside all hope..." (Все упованїе); Glory, Tone 8: "The choir of the Holy Fathers..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 3: "Apostolic traditions..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: The Beatitudes in the Octoechos on 6, and of the Holy Fathers, Odes 3 and 6, on 6; Troparion and Kontakion of the Holy Fathers; Glory, and now, Theotokion: "Protection of Christians...". Prokeimenon, Tone 6: "The righteous shall be glad in the Lord" [LXX Ps. 63:11]; Verse: "Blessed are they whom Thou hast chosen" [LXX Ps. 64:5]. Epistle to Titus [Tit. 3:8–15]: "Child Titus, faithful is the saying, and concerning these things I desire that thou affirm...". Alleluia, Tone 4: "The righteous cried"*[^431] *[Alleluia, Tone 4: "The righteous] cried, [and the Lord heard them]" [LXX Ps. 33:18]. Gospel from Matthew [Mt. 5:14–19]: "Ye are the light of the world...". Kinonikon: "In everlasting memory shall the righteous be" [LXX Ps. 111:6]. And the office of Saint Athenogenes is sung whenever the ecclesiarch wishes.*
*On the 17th day: Of the Holy Great-Martyr Marina; and the Translation of the Relics of our Venerable Father Lazarus of Mount Galesios:*  
*At Vespers: Stichera of the saint on 6; Glory, Idiomelon; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 2: "In the voice of rejoicing..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion: "Thy lamb, O Jesus..." [Tone 4], see [September] 16. At Matins: Canons in the Octoechos, and of the saint on 6. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 3:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Great-Martyr Marina, Tone 3:*  
Today Marina crushes the head of the enemy, having received the crown of victory from heaven; for whom they could not restrain by fierce assault, him she crushed and wounded; therefore she hath been shown forth the ornament of martyrs, together with their praise!  
*At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 1: "As a refined vessel..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: from the canon of the saint, Odes 3 and 6. Prokeimenon: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]. Epistle: see [September] 16 [2 Cor. 6:1–10]. Alleluia: "With patience I waited for the Lord" [LXX Ps. 39:2]. Gospel from Luke*[^432] *Gospel from Luke [Lk. 7:36–50]. Kinonikon: "Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous" [LXX Ps. 32:1].*
*On the 18th day: Of the Holy Martyr Emilian. Alleluia.*  
*On the 19th day: Of Holy Macrina, sister of Basil the Great; and of our holy father Dios: Their office together.*  
*Troparion: "In thee, O mother...", see [September] 25; Glory: "A dweller in the wilderness...", see [September] 29. At Matins: Canon in the Octoechos on 6, and of the saints two. Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of Venerable Dios the Wonderworker, Tone 2:*  
Armed divinely with courage of soul, and having taken up unceasing prayer as a spear, thou didst mightily cut down the demonic legions, O Dios, our wonderworking father, praying unceasingly on behalf of us all!  
*On the 20th day: Of the Holy and Glorious Prophet Elijah:*  
*At Vespers: Ordinary Kathisma psalmody. At "Lord, I have cried": [stichera on 8]; at the Litiya: Tone 1... and 3 idiomela, Tone 2; Glory, Tone 6: "O prophet and herald of Christ..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 4:*  
*Troparion of the Holy Prophet Elijah, Tone 4:*  
An angel in the flesh, the foundation of the prophets, the second forerunner of the coming of Christ, glorious Elijah, who sent down grace from on high unto Elisha*[^433] *to drive away sicknesses and cleanse lepers; therefore unto them that honor him he pours forth healings!*
*At Matins: Canons in the Octoechos, and of the saint on 6; after Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Prophet Elijah, Tone 2:*  
O prophet and herald of the great works of our God, O Elijah of great renown, who by thy word didst hold back the water-flowing clouds, pray on our behalf unto the only Lover of mankind!  
*At the Praises: 3 prosomoia, Tone 8; and 3 idiomela, Tone 1; Glory, Tone 8: "Chief of the prophets..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 4: "Upon the chariot of fire..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: to the prophet from the canon, Odes 3 and 6. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "Thou art a priest forever after the order of Melchizedek" [LXX Ps. 109:4]; Verse: "The Lord said unto my Lord: Sit Thou at My right hand" [LXX Ps. 109:1]. Epistle of James [Jas. 5:10–20]: "Brethren, take as an example of suffering affliction...". Alleluia, Tone 4: "Moses and Aaron among His priests" [LXX Ps. 98:6]. Gospel from Luke [Lk. 4:22–30]: "At that time, the people wondered at the words of grace...". Kinonikon: "In everlasting memory shall the righteous be" [LXX Ps. 111:6].*  
*On the 21st day: Memory of our venerable father John and Symeon the Fool-for-Christ; and of the Holy Prophet Ezekiel:*  
*Troparion: "O God of our fathers...", see [November 20]. Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of Venerable John and Symeon the Fool-for-Christ, Tone 2:*  
Inflamed with the love of Christ, O glorious ones, ye abandoned all things in this earthly life*[^434] *fleeting, and having accepted His yoke and lifted the Cross upon your shoulder, ye became imitators of His life, partakers of His divine glory, O divinely-wise John with marvelous Symeon: pray unceasingly on behalf of us all!*
*On the 22nd day: Of Holy Mary Magdalene, the Myrrh-bearer; and of the Holy Hieromartyr Phocas:*  
*At Vespers: Stichera of the saint on 6; Glory, Tone 6: "Having first beheld..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 8: "Unto Him Who willingly impoverished Himself..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 1:*  
*Troparion of Holy Mary Magdalene, Tone 1:*  
Unto Christ, Who for our sake was born of the Virgin, honorable Magdalene, thou didst follow, keeping His ordinances and laws; therefore rejoicing we celebrate thy most holy memory, praising thee in faith and honoring thee with love!  
*Glory: "And having shared in the ways..." [Troparion of St. Phocas, Tone 4]; Both now, Theotokion. At Matins: Canon in the Octoechos on 6, and of the saints two. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 3, Podoben: "Today the Virgin..." (Дѣва днесь):*  
*Kontakion of Holy Mary Magdalene, Tone 3:*  
Standing, O all-glorious one, at the Cross of the Savior with many others, and weeping tears of praise unto the Mother of the Lord, bringing this offering*[^435] *crying aloud: "What strange marvel is this? He Who upholds all creation hath willed to suffer! Glory to Thy dominion!"*
*At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 4: "From thy youth...", see [fol.] 22; Both now: "Unto Him Who willingly impoverished Himself...". At the Divine Liturgy: from the canon of Mary, Odes 3 and 6. Prokeimenon: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]; Verse: "In the congregations bless ye God" [LXX Ps. 67:27]. Epistle: lection 16 [1 Cor. 9:2–12]. Alleluia: "With patience I waited for the Lord" [LXX Ps. 39:2]. Gospel from Luke [Lk. 8:1–3]. Kinonikon: "Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous" [LXX Ps. 32:1].*  
*On the 23rd day: Of the Holy Martyrs Trophimus and Theophilus and those with them. Alleluia.*  
*On the 24th day: Of the Holy Martyr Christina; and of the Holy Passion-bearing Martyrs Boris and Gleb:*  
*At Vespers: "Blessed is the man" [Kathisma 1]. Stichera of the saints on 6; Glory of the saint [Christina], Tone 2: "As an alabaster flask..."; Both now, Theotokion. Entrance. Prokeimenon. 3 Lections, see [September] 26, written for the Theologian. At the Aposticha: 3 prosomoia of the saint; then Verse: "Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones" [LXX Ps. 115:6]; and idiomelon, Tone 6: "Come, let us praise..."; Glory, Tone 2, of the saint: "Unto the named after Christ..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion of the Martyrs [Tone 4]: "Thy martyrs, O Lord...". At Matins: Troparion of the saints twice; Glory of the saint; Both now, Theotokion. Ordinary Kathisma psalmody*[^436] *and the Polyeleos; Sessional hymns of the saints, Tone 4. Prokeimenon: "Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones" [LXX Ps. 115:6]; Verse: "What shall I render unto the Lord for all that He hath rendered unto me?" [LXX Ps. 115:3]. "Let every breath praise the Lord" [LXX Ps. 150:6]. Gospel from Luke [Lk. 12:2–12]. Psalm 50 [LXX Ps. 50:3]; Verse: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]; and Idiomelon, Tone 8: "Despising bodily pleasures...". Canon of the saint [Christina] with the heirmos on 6, and of the saints [Boris and Gleb] on 8. After Ode 3, Kontakion of the saint [Christina], Tone 4:*
*Kontakion of the Holy Martyr Christina, Tone 4:*  
A radiant dove wast thou shown forth, having golden wings, flying up unto the heavenly heights, O honorable Christina; therefore we celebrate thy glorious feast in faith, venerating the shrine of thy relics, wherefrom divine healings richly flow forth unto all, both unto souls and bodies!  
*Then Sessional hymn of the saint; Glory of the saints; Both now, Theotokion. After Ode 6, Kontakion of the saints, Tone 3, Podoben: "Today the Virgin..." (Дѣва днесь):*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Passion-bearers Boris and Gleb (Roman and David), Tone 3:*  
Today hath shone forth the all-glorious memory of you both, the martyrs of Christ, Roman and David, calling us together unto the praising of Christ our God; therefore running unto the shrine of your relics, we receive the gift of healing*[^437] *for ye are divine physicians! Exapostilarion. At the Praises: 3 prosomoia of the saint [Christina], and 3 of the saints [Boris and Gleb]; then Verse: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]; and Idiomelon of the saint, Tone 5: "Thy virginal..."; then another Verse: "Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones" [LXX Ps. 115:6]; and Idiomelon, Tone 6: "Today, ye faithful..."; Glory, Tone 8: "The first-newly-illumined [rulers]..."; Both now, Theotokion. Great Doxology. Troparion of the saints; Glory, and now, Theotokion. Litany, Dismissal, and the 1st Hour. At the Divine Liturgy: from the canon of the saints, Ode 3 on 4, and of the saint, Ode 6 on 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "Unto the saints that are in His earth hath the Lord been marvelous" [LXX Ps. 15:3]; Verse: "I foresaw the Lord always before me" [LXX Ps. 15:8]. Epistle to the Romans [Rom. 8:28–39]: "Brethren, we know that to them that love God all things work together for good...". Alleluia: "The righteous cried, and the Lord heard them" [LXX Ps. 33:18]. Gospel from John [Jn. 15:17–16:2]: "The Lord said unto His disciples: These things I command you...". Kinonikon: "Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous" [LXX Ps. 32:1].*
*On the 25th day: The Falling Asleep of Holy Anna, Mother of the Most Holy Theotokos; and of the holy women Olympias and Eupraxia:*  
*At Vespers: Stichera of the saint on 6; Glory, and now, Tone 8: "Out of a barren womb...". At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 2: "Unto the true Lover..."; Both now, Tone 8: "Come, all creation...". Troparion, Tone 4:*  
*Troparion of the Falling Asleep of Holy Anna, Tone 4:*  
The Bearer of Life didst thou bear in thy womb, the pure Mother of God, O divinely-wise Anna; therefore unto the heavenly reception*[^438] *where is the dwelling of them that rejoice in glory, rejoicing now hast thou passed over, asking cleansing of transgressions for them that honor thee with love, O venerable and ever-blessed one!*
*At Matins: Canon in the Octoechos, and of the saints two. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of the Falling Asleep of Holy Anna, Tone 2:*  
We celebrate the memory of the forebears of Christ, in faith asking their aid, that all may be delivered from every tribulation, who cry aloud: Our God is with us, Who hath glorified them even as He was well-pleased!  
*At the Aposticha: Glory, and now: "Out of a barren womb...". At the Divine Liturgy: from the canon of the saints, Odes 3 and 6. Prokeimenon: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]; Verse: "In the congregations bless ye God" [LXX Ps. 67:27]. Epistle: see [September] 9 [Heb. 9:1–7]: "Brethren, the first tabernacle had ordinances of divine service...". Alleluia: "The salvation of the righteous is of the Lord" [LXX Ps. 36:39]. Gospel from Luke [Lk. 1:39–49, 56 or Lk. 8:16–21]. Kinonikon: "In everlasting memory shall the righteous be" [LXX Ps. 111:6].*  
*On the 26th day: Of the Holy Hieromartyr Hermolaus and those with him. Alleluia.*  
*On the 27th day: Of the Holy Great-Martyr and Healer Panteleimon:*  
*At Vespers: At "Lord, I have cried": 3 prosomoia of the saint, and 3 idiomela, Tone 2; Glory, Tone 8: "Having loved..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 3:*  
*Troparion of the Holy Great-Martyr Panteleimon, Tone 3:*  
Passion-bearer of Christ, Panteleimon, entreat the Merciful God*[^439] *that He grant remission of sins unto our souls.*[^440]
*At Matins: Canons in the Octoechos, and of the saint on 6; after Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 5, Idiomelon:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Great-Martyr Panteleimon, Tone 5, Idiomelon:*  
Being an imitator of the Merciful One, and having received the grace of healings, O passion-bearer and martyr of Christ,[^441] by thy prayers heal the infirmities of our souls, and the fire of the tempter's snares; save those who faithfully cry aloud unto Thee!  
*At the Praises: stichera of the saint on 6; Glory, Tone 4: "Today hath shone forth..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: 3 verses: "The righteous shall flourish like a palm tree" [LXX Ps. 91:13]; idiomela, Tone 1: "A noble contest..."; Glory, Tone 5: "Come together, ye lovers of martyrs..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: from the canon, Odes 3 and 6. Prokeimenon: "God is marvelous in His saints" [LXX Ps. 67:36]; Verse: "In the congregations bless ye God" [LXX Ps. 67:27]. Epistle: lection 25 [2 Tim. 2:1–10]. Alleluia, Tone 4: "The righteous shall flourish like a palm tree" [LXX Ps. 91:13]. Gospel from John, lection 52 [Jn. 15:17–16:2] after Pascha. Kinonikon: "In everlasting memory shall the righteous be" [LXX Ps. 111:6].*[^442]  
*On the 28th day: Of the Holy Apostles Prochorus, Nicanor, Timon, and Parmenas:*  
*Troparion: "Holy Apostles..." [Tone 3]. Kontakion, Tone 1:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Apostles Prochorus, Nicanor, Timon, and Parmenas, Tone 1:*  
Honorable deacons and eyewitnesses of the Word, and chosen vessels of faith were ye shown forth, O Nicanor, and Prochorus, Parmenas*[^443] *and glorious Timon; therefore today we celebrate your sacred memory, blessing you in gladness of heart!*
*On the 29th day: Of the Holy Martyr Callinicus. Alleluia.*  
*On the 30th day: Of the Holy Apostles Silas, Silvanus, and those with them:*  
*Troparion: "Holy Apostles..." [Tone 3]. Kontakion, Tone 4:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Apostles Silas and Silvanus, Tone 4:*  
Ye were shown forth as branches of the vine of Christ, bearing clusters of grapes in virtues, O wise ones, pouring forth for us the wine of salvation; which receiving, we are filled with gladness, and we celebrate your honorable memory, wherein entreat ye that remission of sins be given unto us, O apostles![^444]  
*On the 31st day: Of the holy and glorious Eudocimus; and the Forefeast of the Precious Cross:*  
*At Vespers: stichera 3 of the Cross, and 3 of the saint; Glory of the Cross, Tone 1: "Lo, the night..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 6: "Thou art my shelter..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 4:*  
*Troparion of Righteous Eudocimus of Cappadocia, Tone 4:*  
He Who called thee from the earth unto the eternal mansions preserves even after death thy body unharmed, O holy one; for thou in temperance*[^445] *and purity didst lead thy life, O blessed one, not having defiled the flesh; therefore with boldness pray unto Christ God that our souls may be saved!*
*Glory, Both now, of the Cross, Tone 1: "Save, O Lord, Thy people". At Matins: Troparion of the Cross twice; Glory, of the saint; Both now, of the Cross. Kathisma in the Octoechos on 6; of the Cross on 4; and of the saint on 4. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of Righteous Eudocimus of Cappadocia, Tone 2:*  
Desiring the highest things, thou wast joined with the highest today, and as a fiery chariot by divine ascents of virtues didst thou fashion thy soul, O all-blessed Eudocimus; and having lived upon earth as one bodiless, thou didst please the Creator of all!  
*At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 6, of the Cross: "The prophetic voice..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: of the Cross, and of the saint.*  
*On that day orthodox Christians cease eating meat.*[^446]  
*From the first day of August, the more devout Christians eat no fish until the Falling Asleep of the Holy Theotokos.*

*The Month of August hath 31 days; the day hath 13 hours, and the night 11.*  
*On the 1st day: The Procession of the Precious and Life-giving Cross; and of the Holy Seven Maccabee Martyrs, and their teacher Eleazar, and their mother Solomonia:*[^447]  
*Before the ringing of the bells, the priest enters with the superior of the church and with the candle-bearer into the skeuophylakion, and taking up the Precious Wood with candles and with a censer, singing the Troparion: "Save, O Lord, Thy people", and they bring it into the church and place it upon the Holy Table, and they kindle a candle by the Precious Wood for the whole evening. The ordinary kathisma psalmody. At "Lord, I have cried": stichera 3 of the Cross, and 3 of the saint; Glory, Tone 8: "Holy Maccabees"; Both now, of the Cross, Tone 8: "He Whom of old...". At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 8: "The souls of the righteous..."; Both now, of the Cross: "The voice of Thy Prophet...". Troparion: "By the sufferings of the Holy..."; (see fol. 24)[^448] Glory, Both now: "Save, O Lord, Thy people". At Matins: Troparion of the Cross On that day is sung the office of Saint Maximus; stichera of both... Glory, Tone 6: "Venerable father..."; Both now, of the feast. At the Aposticha: all of the feast. Troparion... (see fol. 11);[^449] Glory, Both now, of the feast. At Matins: canons of the feast on 6, and two... After Ode 3, Kontakion of the saint, Tone 2:*
*Kontakion of Venerable Maximus the Confessor and His Companions, Tone 2:*  
Divinely-wise and valiant in blessed yokes, let us praise your glorious ones as firm preachers of piety and radiant lovers of suffering, and being divine zealots, crowning them with hymns and praises![^450]  
*On the 13th day: Of our Venerable Father Maximus the Confessor. On that day the Feast of the Transfiguration of the Lord is given back [the Apodosis of the Transfiguration]:*  
*At Vespers: ordinary kathisma psalmody; stichera idiomela on 4, Tone 4; and on 6; Glory, Both now: "Prefiguring Thy Resurrection, O Christ God..."; Prokeimenon of the day, without entrance. At the Aposticha: idiomela of the feast; Glory, Both now, Theotokion, Tone 6: "He Who of old spake through symbols unto Moses upon Mount Sinai...". At Matins: Troparion of the feast 3 times; ordinary kathisma psalmody; Sessional hymns of the feast... At Ode 6, Katavasia: "Cross did Moses trace" until the feast is given back [during the Afterfeast of the Transfiguration]: after Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the feast; Glory, Both now, of the feast. After Ode 6, Kontakion of the feast. At the Aposticha: of the feast; Glory, Both now, Tone 5: "The Fulfiller of the Law and Prophets...". At the Divine Liturgy: of the feast, Ode 3 on 4, and of the saint, Ode 6 on 4. Prokeimenon: "Precious in the sight of the Lord is the death of His venerable ones" [LXX Ps. 115:6]; Verse: "What shall I render unto the Lord" [LXX Ps. 115:3]. Epistle: (see fol. 27);[^451] Alleluia: "The righteous shall flourish like a palm tree" [LXX Ps. 91:13]. Gospel from John, see [fol.] 3 [Jn. 15:17–16:2] after Pascha. Kinonikon: "In everlasting memory shall the righteous be" [LXX Ps. 111:6].*
*On the 8th day: Of our holy father Emilian, Bishop of Cyzicus. Alleluia.*  
*On the 9th day: Of the Holy Apostle Matthias:*  
*At Vespers: of the feast and of the saint. Troparion: "Holy Apostle..." [Tone 3]. Kontakion, Tone 2:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Apostle Matthias, Tone 2:*  
An unwaning sun wast thou shown forth, cleaving unto the Great Sun; therefore was he also illumined who was seeking in the darkness of ignorance, driving away infirmities from our souls and bodies, O Matthias, eyewitness of mysteries and servant of Christ in truth; praying always, cease not on behalf of us all![^452]  
*Common service of the Apostles.*  
*On the 10th day: Of the Holy Martyr and Archdeacon Laurence. Alleluia.*  
*On the 11th day: Of the Holy Martyr Euplus. At Vespers: of the feast and of the saint. Troparion and Kontakion of the feast.*  
*On the 12th day: Of the Holy Martyrs Photius and Anicletus twice; Glory, of the saints; Both now, of the Cross. The ordinary kathisma psalmody; and reading in the Theologian [St. Gregory Nazianzen], as is customary. And in the refectory is read "Prepitan" concerning the saints. Canons: in the Octoechos on 4; and of the saints on 4; and of the Cross on 6. Katavasia: "Cross did Moses trace". The canon of the Cross is sung after, for the sake of the Katavasia. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn, Tone 2, Podoben: "Seeking the highest..." (Вышнихъ ищя):*[^453]
*Sessional Hymn of the Holy Maccabees, Tone 2, Podoben: "Seeking the highest..." (Вышнихъ ищя):*  
O sevenfold pillars of the Wisdom of God, and seven-branched lamps of the divine Light, all-wise Maccabees, great martyrs before the martyrs, with them pray ye unto the God of all that they who honor you may be saved![^454]  
*Then Sessional hymn; Glory, of the Cross; Both now, Theotokion. After Ode 6, Kontakion of the Cross. After the Kathisma [at Matins], at the Praises: stichera 3 of the Cross, and of the saints 3 idiomela: 1st, Tone 1: "O much-suffering mother..."; 2nd, Tone 4: "Seven pillars..."; 3rd, Tone 5: "Keepers of the Law..."; Glory, Tone 4, that concerning the Maccabees; Both now, of the Cross, Podoben: "Thou Who didst willingly ascend..." [Tone 4].*  
*The Great Doxology. While the brethren are singing, the priest vests in priestly vestments, and comes with a censer and with a lamp unto the Holy Table, and censes the Holy Table and the Precious Wood, and*[^455] *and the priest having vested, singing the Troparion: "Save, O Lord, Thy people", bowing in reverence.*
*On the 2nd day: The Translation of the Relics of the Holy Protomartyr and Archdeacon Stephen:*[^456]  
*At Vespers: ordinary kathisma psalmody; stichera of the saint 3, and 3 idiomela, Tone 2; Glory, Tone 6: "The first leader..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 8: "Rejoice in the Lord..."; Both now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 4:*  
*Troparion of the Holy Protomartyr and Archdeacon Stephen, Tone 4:*  
With a royal diadem was thy crown adorned from the sufferings which thou didst endure for Christ God, O first-sufferer of martyrs; for thou didst denounce the fury of the Jews, and sawest thy Savior at the right hand of the Father; Him therefore pray thou unceasingly that we all may be saved!  
*At Matins: canon in the Octoechos, and of the saint on 6. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 6, Idiomelon:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Protomartyr and Archdeacon Stephen, Tone 6, Idiomelon:*  
First wast thou sown upon earth by the Heavenly Husbandman, O all-praised one; first didst thou pour out thy blood for Christ upon the earth, O blessed one; first wast thou adorned by Him with a crown of victory in the heavens, as the first-risen among sufferers, the crown-bearer of disciples, O first-sufferer![^457]  
*At the Praises: prosomoia and of the saint on 4; Glory, Tone 4: "Good Stephen..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Aposticha: Glory, Tone 5: "First-martyr..."; Both now, Theotokion. At the Divine Liturgy: Prokeimenon: "The righteous shall rejoice in the Lord" [LXX Ps. 63:11]; Verse: "Hear, O God, my prayer" [LXX Ps. 63:2]. Epistle from the Acts [Acts 6:8–7:5, 47–60]. Alleluia: "The righteous shall flourish like a palm tree" [LXX Ps. 91:13]. Gospel from Luke, lection 53 [Lk. 12:32–40]: "The Lord spake this parable: A certain man...". Kinonikon: "Into all the earth [their sound is gone out]" [LXX Ps. 18:5].*
*On the 3rd day: Of our Venerable Fathers Isaac, Dalmatus, and Faustus. Alleluia.*  
*On the 4th day: Of the Holy Seven Youths who were in Ephesus:*  
*Kontakion, Tone 4:*  
*Kontakion of the Holy Seven Youths of Ephesus, Tone 4:*  
Having despised the corruptible things in the world and received incorruptible gifts, ye remained incorrupt after dying, wherefrom also ye arose after many years, having buried all the ferocious deceit of deceivers; therefore praising them today, we the faithful in hymns give thanks unto Christ![^458]  
*On the 5th day: The Forefeast of the Transfiguration of the Lord; and of the Holy Martyr Eusignius:*[^459]  
*At Vespers: at "Lord, I have cried": prosomoia 3 of the forefeast, and 3 of the saint; Glory, Both now, Tone 5: "Come, let us ascend..."; At the Aposticha: Glory, Both now, Tone 2 "...upon Mount Tabor". Troparion, Tone 4:*
*Troparion of the Forefeast of the Transfiguration, Tone 4:*  
Let us go forth to meet the Transfiguration of Christ, brightly celebrating the forefeast, ye faithful, let us cry aloud: The time of divine joy of souls hath drawn near, the Master goes up upon Mount Tabor to flash forth the beauty of His Godhead![^460]  
*At Matins: canon in the Octoechos on 4; and of the saint on 4; and of the forefeast on 6. And in whatever ode there are triodia, the Octoechos is omitted, and the saint's is sung on 4, and the forefeast on 4, and the triodia on 6. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the saint; Glory, Both now, of the forefeast. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou didst appear today..." (Явился еси днесь):*  
*Kontakion of the Forefeast of the Transfiguration, Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou didst appear today..." (Явился еси днесь):*  
By Thy divine Transfiguration today human nature hath been divinely illumined, and leaps for joy, crying aloud: Christ is transfigured, saving all!  
*At the Aposticha: Glory, Both now, Tone 5: "The Fulfiller of the Law...".*  
*On the 6th day: The Holy Transfiguration of Our Lord God and Savior Jesus Christ:*[^461]  
*An All-Night Vigil. At Little Vespers: stichera 4 of the feast, Tone 4: "Before Thy Crucifixion..."; Glory And now, Tone 6: "Prefiguring Thy Holy Transfiguration..."; and on the Aposticha, Idiomela 3, Tone 1; Glory, and now, Tone 6: "Taking Peter and James..." Troparion, Tone 7:*[^462]

*Troparion:*
Thou wast transfigured upon the mountain, O Christ God, revealing Thy glory unto Thy disciples as far as they could bear it. Let Thine everlasting Light shine forth also upon us sinners, through the prayers of the Mother of God, O Giver of Light, glory to Thee!

*At Great Vespers:*
*Blessed is the man. On "Lord, I have cried," we sing 8 verses, with Idiomela 4 of the feast, Podoben: Tone 4, "Before Thy Precious Cross"; Glory, and now, Tone 6: "Prefiguring..." Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day, and three readings:*[^463]
*Two from Exodus: "The Lord said unto Moses: Come up to Me into the mount..." [Ex. 24:12–18]; "The Lord spake unto Moses face to face..." [Ex. 33:11–23]; the third from the Prophecy of Kings: the ascent of Elijah [3 Kings 19:3–16].*
*At the Litiya: Idiomela of the feast 3, Tone 2; Glory, Tone 5: "Come, let us ascend..."; And now, Tone 5: "The law and the prophets..." On the Aposticha: Idiomela 3, Tone 1, Tone 5, and Tone 8, the same tones: "For Thou, Tabor and Hermon..."; Glory, and now, Tone 6: "Taking Peter and James..." Troparion of the feast thrice. Blessing of the loaves.*

*Blessing of loaves, and reading of the encomium of the feast.*
*At Matins:*
*Troparion of the feast thrice. Ordinary psalmody [Kathismata]. At the Polyeleos: Sessional hymns of the feast, and reading from the books of St. John Chrysostom, Homilies 56 and 57.*[^464] *Gradual, Tone 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "Tabor and Hermon shall rejoice in Thy name" [Ps. 88:13]. Verse: "Thine are the heavens" [Ps. 88:12]. "Let every breath..." Gospel from Luke [9:28–36]: "At that time, Jesus took Peter [and John and James]..." And we kiss the Gospel. "Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ" we do not say. Two canons: of master Cosmas [Tone 4] and of master John [Tone 8]; heirmoi twice, and troparia four times. Katabasia: "Tracing the Cross," both choirs together. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the feast. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 7, Idiomelon:*[^465]

*Kontakion:*
Thou wast transfigured upon the mountain, O Christ God, and Thy disciples beheld Thy glory as far as they were able, that when they should see Thee crucified, they might understand Thy voluntary Passion, and proclaim unto the world that Thou art truly the Radiance of the Father!

*At Ode 9, candles are given to the brethren. "More honorable" is not sung. Exapostilarion of the feast thrice. On the Praises, Stichera again 3 of the feast on 4, Tone 4: "Called from on high"; Glory, and now, Tone 8: "Christ took Peter..." Great Doxology. Troparion of the feast, litany, dismissal, and holy oil is given to the brethren. At the Hours: Troparion and Kontakion of the feast. At the Liturgy: from the canons, Ode 3 and Ode 6. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "O Lord, how magnified are Thy works; in wisdom hast Thou made them all" [Ps. 103:24]. Epistle from the Catholic Epistle of Peter [2 Pet. 1:10–19]: "Brethren, give diligence to make your calling and election sure..." Alleluia, Tone 8: "Thine are the heavens, and Thine is the earth" [Ps. 88:12]; verse: "Blessed is the people that know the joyful sound" [Ps. 88:16]. Gospel from Matthew [17:1–9]: "At that time, Jesus takes Peter, James, and John His brother..." Communion Hymn: "O Lord, in the light of Thy countenance shall they walk, and in Thy name shall they rejoice unto ages [Ps. 88:16–17]. Alleluia."*[^466]

*It is meet to know that according to tradition, on the celebrated feast of the Transfiguration it is appointed to eat grapes, which receiving blessing through prayer at the conclusion of the Divine Liturgy, and after the breaking of bread, that is, after the distribution of the antidoron, there is said: Troparion, "Thou wast transfigured..."; Glory, and now, Kontakion: "Thou wast transfigured upon the mountain..."; while this is being said, the priest censes. Then the priest [says]: "Let us pray to the Lord."*[^467]

*And we [chant]: "Lord, have mercy." And the priest [reads] this prayer:*[^468]

*Prayer for the Blessing of Grapes:*
O Lord our God, Who didst formerly adorn the earth with beautiful flowers, and having enriched it with marvelous variety, didst beautify it in every aspect; and Who again, having established the earth by the four seasons, and by flowing streams of dew, dew-dropping and cooling it from the air, and by the warmth of the sun hast been well pleased to bring these fruits unto ripeness: do Thou Thyself bless these grapes with Thy grace, that they may be unto us for gladness, and for thanksgiving without physical excess, and for the strength of Thy dominion; and that for this cause we may offer praise unto the cleansing of our offenses, through the once-offered holy and life-creating Body and Blood of our Lord Jesus Christ, Thine Only-begotten Son, with Whom Thou art blessed, together with Thy Most-holy, Good, and Life-creating Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen.

*And let this be known: If any of the brethren taste of the grapes prior to such feast let him receive a penance, namely, by this penance not to taste grapes for the entire month of August, as a transgressor of the ordinance, that he may learn to observe these things. And let this tradition be kept always also by the vinedressers. And grapes at the refectory after the feast are served thrice in the week, the brethren tasting thereof at the noon meal; and likewise let it be done with other fruits in their proper season.*[^469]

*The Holy Venerable-Martyr Dometius [August 7]:*[^470]
*At Vespers, no psalmody [Kathisma]. On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera again 3 of the feast, Tone 4: "Called from on high"; and of the Saint, Tone 8. Glory, and now: "Prefiguring..." Entrance. Prokeimenon, Tone 7: "Our God is in heaven and on earth" [Ps. 113:11]; verse: "In the exodus of Israel" [Ps. 113:1]; "The sea beheld and fled" [Ps. 113:3]. On the Aposticha, Stichera again of the feast: "Thou hast ascended..."; Glory, and now: "Come, let us ascend..." Troparion: "Thy martyr..." Glory, and now, of the feast. At Matins: Troparion of the feast twice; Glory, [Saint], and now, of the feast. Sessional hymn of the feast. Canon of the feast with the heirmos on 6, and of the Saint on 6; and Biblical odes are not sung.*

*After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the feast twice; and after Ode 6, Kontakion and Ikos of the feast. At Ode 9, "More honorable" is not sung. Exapostilarion of the feast. On the Praises, Stichera of the feast, Tone 8: "From the darkness of the law..." on 4; Glory, and now: "Christ took Peter..." Great Doxology. Troparion of the feast, litany, catechetical reading, dismissal. At the Liturgy: from both canons, Ode 9 with heirmoi. At the Beatitudes: Troparion of the feast; Glory, and now, Kontakion of the feast. Prokeimenon, Alleluia, and Communion Hymn of the feast; and Epistle and Gospel of the day.*

*Forefeast of the Falling Asleep of the Most Holy Theotokos, and the Holy Prophet Micah [August 14]:*[^471]
*At Vespers, Stichera 3 of the Forefeast, and 3 of the Saint; Glory, and now, Tone 4: "The all-honorable Falling Asleep..." On the Aposticha, Glory, and now, Tone 2: "He Who is in the highest..." Troparion, Tone 4:*

*Troparion of the Forefeast:*
O ye people, dance beforehand, clapping your hands with faith and love, gather yourselves together today, rejoicing brightly, shouting all together with gladness; for the Mother of God is about to pass over gloriously from earthly things unto the heights; whom with hymns as the Mother of God we ever glorify!

*+ ...and the brethren chanting, and he goes around inside the monastery, and forward into the Igumen's cell, and into the refectory, and into the cellarer's office, and into the cookhouse, into the bakery, into the granary, into the cellars, and into all the cells of the brethren; and having returned, we sing the First Hour, and there is the complete dismissal.*[^472]
*At the Liturgy: the Beatitudes, Ode 3 on 4, and of the Cross, Ode 6 on 4; Troparion of the Cross, of the Saint; Glory, Kontakion of the Saint; and now, Kontakion of the Cross. Prokeimenon, Tone 2: "Save, O Lord, Thy people"; verse: "Unto Thee, O Lord..." And the other Prokeimenon for the Saints. Epistle for the Cross, lection 125 [1 Cor. 1:18–24]; and of the Saints, lection to All Saints [Heb. 11:33–40]. Alleluia, Tone 1: "Remember Thy congregation..." [Ps. 73:2]; verse: "God is our King..." [Ps. 73:12]; and the other: "The righteous cried..." [Ps. 33:18]. Gospel for the Cross, from John, lection 60 [John 19:6–11, 13–20, 25–28, 30–35]; and Gospel for the Saints, lection to All Saints [Matt. 10:32–33, 37–38; 19:27–30]. Communion Hymn: "The light of Thy countenance is signed upon us" [Ps. 4:7]; another: "Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous" [Ps. 32:1].*[^473] [^474]
*On that same day, after Vespers, the priest takes up the Precious Wood, and it is borne into the treasury, with candles and with a censer.*

*He takes up the Precious Wood with the dish upon his head, and goes forth by the northern side out of the altar, two great candles going before him with a censer; and he comes unto the Royal Doors, and there stands awaiting the conclusion of the Trisagion. And the Trisagion being concluded, the deacon proclaims: "Wisdom! Stand upright!" The brethren chant the troparion: "Save, O Lord, Thy people," chanting it once. And the priest comes, bearing the Precious Wood, before the Holy Doors to a prepared table, and lays it upon the table, and censes. Water having been prepared in a vessel, troparia are chanted in order; the priest blesses the water. After the blessing of the water and the sprinkling of the sanctified water, he makes veneration to the Precious Wood, in ordinary order, bowing down thrice; after this, litany, dismissal.*[^475]
*And when it is the day of the procession to the water, having returned, he makes veneration to the Precious Wood; litany, dismissal; and after the dismissal, the Precious Cross goes forth with the blessed water, the priest being in vestments, + At Matins:*
*Canon in the Oktoechos on 6, and of the Forefeast on 4, and of the Saint on 4. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the Saint; Glory, and now, of the Forefeast. After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 4:*

*Kontakion of the Forefeast:*
In thy glorious memory the universe, adorned with immaterial light, spiritually with gladness cries unto thee: Rejoice, O Virgin, boast of Christians!

*On the Aposticha, Glory, and now, Tone 3: "Come, ye ends of the earth..."*

*The Falling Asleep of the Most Holy Theotokos [August 15]:*[^476]
*We celebrate on this day. All-Night Vigil. At Small Vespers: Stichera of the feast on 4; Glory, and now, Idiomelon of the feast; on the Aposticha, of the feast. Troparion, Tone 1:*[^477]

*Troparion:*
In giving birth Thou didst preserve Thy virginity; in falling asleep Thou didst not forsake the world, O Mother of God. Thou hast passed over unto life, being the Mother of Life, and by Thy prayers Thou dost deliver our souls from death!

*At Great Vespers:*
*Blessed is the man. On "Lord, I have cried," we sing 8 verses: Stichera 4 of the feast, Tone 1: "O marvelous wonder!" and the other 3, the same twice; Glory, and now, Tone 1: "By the divine command..." Entrance. Prokeimenon of the day.*

*Three readings for the feast. At the Litiya: Idiomela 3, Tone 1, Tone 2, and Tone 3; Glory, Tone 5: "Come, ye lovers of the feasts"; And now: "Sing, O ye people." On the Aposticha, Idiomela, Tone 4; and speak the verses: "Arise, O Lord, into Thy rest" [Ps. 131:8]; "The Lord hath sworn unto David" [Ps. 131:11]; Glory, and now, Tone 4: "When thou wentest forth, O Mother of God..."*
*After the Trisagion: the troparion of the feast is said thrice. Blessing of the loaves, and reading in Metaphrastes: the Life of the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^478]
*At Matins:*
*Troparion of the feast thrice. Ordinary psalmody [Kathismata]. At the Polyeleos: Sessional hymns of the feast. Gradual, Tone 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "I will remember Thy name" [Ps. 44:18]; verse: "Give ear, O daughter, and consider, and incline thine ear" [Ps. 44:11]. "Let every breath..." Gospel from Luke [1:39–49, 56]: "In those days, Mary arose..." Sticheron of the feast, Tone 6: "When the passing..." Two canons: of master Cosmas [Tone 1] and of master John [Tone 4]; heirmoi twice, and troparia on 12; afterwards the heirmos of both canons both choirs together once. After Ode 3, Hypakoe: "All generations shall call thee blessed." After Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 2.*

*Kontakion of the Falling Asleep of the Most Holy Theotokos [Tone 2]:*[^479]
In prayers the sleepless Mother of God, and in intercessions an unfailing hope, neither the grave nor death could hold; for as the Mother of Life, He Who dwelt in Her ever-virgin womb translated Her unto life!

*At Ode 9, candles are given to the brethren. "More honorable" we do not sing. Exapostilarion of the feast thrice. On the Praises, we sing 3 of the feast, Tone 4, on 6; Glory, and now, Tone 6: "On Thine immortal..." Great Doxology. Troparion of the feast; litany, dismissal; and holy oil is given to the brethren.*
*At the Liturgy: for the feast, from the canons, Ode 3 and Ode 6. Prokeimenon, Tone 3: Song of the Mother of God: "My soul doth magnify the Lord" [Luke 1:46]; Verse: "For He hath regarded [the low estate of His handmaiden]" [Luke 1:48]. Epistle to the Philippians [2:5–11]: "Brethren, let this mind be in you..." Alleluia, Tone 8: "Arise, O Lord, into Thy rest" [Ps. 131:8]; Verse: "The Lord hath sworn unto David" [Ps. 131:11]. Gospel from Luke [10:38–42; 11:27–28]: "At that time, Jesus entered into a certain village..." Communion Hymn: "I will take the cup of salvation [and call upon the name of the Lord]" [Ps. 115:4].*[^480]
*If this feast is celebrated for 9 days, the rule concerning the afterfeast is written in the chapters [of the Typikon].*[^481]

*The Holy Martyr Diomedes, and the Translation of the Icon Not Made with Hands of our Lord Jesus Christ, the Holy Mandylion [August 16]:*[^482]
*At Vespers, we do not recite psalmody [Kathisma]. On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera 3 to the Mandylion, and 3 of the Saint; Glory, and now, of the feast; on the Aposticha, of the feast; Glory, to the Mandylion; And now, of the feast. Troparion, Tone 2:*[^483]

*Troparion of the Holy Mandylion:*
We venerate Thy most pure Icon, O Good One, asking forgiveness of our transgressions, O Christ God; for of Thine own will Thou wast pleased to ascend the Cross in the flesh, to deliver them whom Thou hadst created from the bondage of the enemy. Therefore thankfully we cry unto Thee: Having filled all things with joy, O our tender-hearted Savior, Who didst come to save the world!

*Glory, and now, of the feast. At Matins: Troparion of the feast twice; Glory, to the Mandylion; And now, of the feast. Sessional hymn of the feast. Canon to the Icon with the heirmos on 6, of the feast on 4, and of the Saint on 4. After Ode 3, Kontakion of the feast; after Ode 6, Kontakion to the Mandylion, Tone 2:*[^484]

*Kontakion of the Holy Mandylion:*
He Who descended from heaven for the compassions of mercy, O tender-hearted Savior, Thou hast today given the honorable image of Thy most pure flesh unto the city that honors Thee and unto the Christian people as a treasure as a steadfast full armor, from which drawing sanctification, with fervent faith we embrace it!

*On the Aposticha, all of the feast.*
*The Holy Martyr Myron [August 17]: the singing together [with the feast]; and we do not say the biblical odes until the Apodosis of the feast. The Holy Martyrs Florus and Laurus [August 18]: the singing together with the feast. And the Saints: The Holy Martyr Andrew Stratelates and those with him, two thousand five hundred and fifty-three [August 19].*[^485]
*The Holy Prophet Samuel [August 20]: the singing together. Troparion: "Of Thy prophet..." Tone 1; Glory, and now, of the feast. Kontakion of the Saint, Tone 3:*[^486]

*Kontakion of the Holy Prophet Samuel:*
As a precious and honorable gift before thy conception thou wast given unto God, and from thy youth having served Him as an angel, O all-blessed one, thou wast granted to foretell future things. Therefore we cry unto thee: Rejoice, O prophet of God, Samuel, great high priest!

*The Holy Apostle Thaddaeus, and the Martyr Bassa [August 21]. The Holy Martyr Agathonicus [August 22]: on that day is chanted according to the weekly tone [of the Oktoechos]; Stichera of both on 4 of the Saint; and now, of the feast; on the Aposticha, all of the feast; Troparion*[^487] *"Thy martyrs, O Lord..." Glory, and now, of the feast. At Matins: Troparion of the feast twice; Glory, of the Saint; And now, of the feast. Sessional hymns of the feast. Canon of the feast on 6, and of the Saints two [canons]. After Ode 3, Kontakion of the Saint, Tone 4, Podoben: "The angelic choir":*[^488]

*Kontakion of the Holy Martyr Agathonicus:*
Having acquired a noble calling, O divinely-wise one, thou didst turn deceitful men from unbelief, fearing no torments, O glorious Agathonicus; therefore thou hast become an inheritor of good things, and hast received with those who suffered with thee a worthy crown!

*Then Sessional hymn of the Saint; Glory, and now, of the feast; after Ode 6, Kontakion and Ikos [of the feast]; on the Aposticha, all of the feast.*
*The Holy Martyr Lupus; on that day is the Apodosis of the feast [August 23]:*[^489]
*At Vespers: ordinary psalmody [Kathisma]. On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the feast on 6, Tone 1: "O marvelous wonder!"; Glory, and now: "By the divine command"; if it be Friday evening: And now, Dogmatic Theotokion of Tone 1, of the current tone; Prokeimenon of the day; without entrance and without readings; on the Aposticha, of the feast, Tone 4; Glory, and now: "When Thou wentest forth, O Mother of God..."*
*At Matins: Troparion of the feast thrice; Sessional hymns of the feast; two canons of the feast, one with the heirmos on 8, and the other with the heirmos on 6.*

*Katabasia: "I shall open my mouth," both choirs together. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the feast; after Ode 6, Kontakion and Ikos. At Ode 9, "More honorable" we do not recite in psalmody. Exapostilarion of the feast twice. On the Praises, we sing 3 of the feast, Tone 4, on 4; Glory, and now, Tone 6: "On Thine immortal..." Great Doxology. Troparion of the feast; litany, catechetical instruction, dismissal. At the Liturgy: of the feast, from both canons both 9th Odes with heirmoi on 8; Troparion of the feast; Glory, and now, Kontakion of the feast; Prokeimenon, Alleluia, Communion Hymn of the feast; and Epistle and Gospel of the day.*
*The Holy Apostle Eutyches, disciple of St. John the Theologian [August 24]:*[^490]
*Troparion and Prokeimenon... Tone 3; Glory, and now, Kontakion, Tone 3:*

*Kontakion of the Holy Apostle Eutyches:*
A fellow-partaker with the Apostles and the beauty of the saints, as Eutyches thou didst glorify the apostolic rank; thou didst shine forth as a star, enlightening all, and didst dispel the deep night of godlessness; therefore we honor thee as truly a divine initiate of Christ!

*The Translation of the Relics of the Holy Apostle Bartholomew [August 25], and of the Holy*[^491] *Apostle Titus, disciple of the Holy Apostle Paul: the service of both together. Troparion: "Holy Apostle..." Glory, and now, Theotokion. At Matins: Canon in the Oktoechos on 6, and to the Saints two [canons]. After Ode 3, Kontakion to Bartholomew, and Hypakoe; after Ode 6, Kontakion to Titus, Tone 2:*[^492]

*Kontakion of the Holy Apostle Titus:*
Thou wast revealed as a fellow-worker with Paul, O Apostle; with him thou didst proclaim the Word unto us by divine grace, O initiate Titus, all-blessed; therefore we cry unto thee: Cease not praying for us all!

*At the Liturgy: Prokeimenon: "Into all the earth [their sound hath gone forth]" [Ps. 18:5]; Verse: "The heavens declare [the glory of God]" [Ps. 18:2]. Epistle to Titus [1:1–4]: "Paul, a servant of God, and an apostle of the Lord Jesus Christ, unto Titus, mine own child..." Alleluia: "The heavens shall confess [Thy wonders]" [Ps. 88:6]. Gospel from Matthew, lection 23 [Matt. 5:14–19]: "The Lord said: Ye are the light of the world..." Communion Hymn: "Into all the earth."*
*The Holy Martyrs Adrian and Natalia [August 26]:*[^493]
*Two Idiomela, Tone 1 and Tone 6; Troparion: "Thy martyrs, O Lord..." Kontakion, Tone 4:*

*Kontakion of the Holy Martyrs Adrian and Natalia:*
The divine memory of the martyrs has shone forth, enlightening brightly all ends of the earth today, crying with gladness: Thou art the rejoicing of martyrs!

*Epigraphic marginal gloss on folio 214r: "On this day is celebrated the feast of the Meeting of the Icon of our Most Pure Lady Theotokos, which is called Vladimirskaya."*[^494]

*Our Venerable Father Poemen [August 27]:*[^495]
*Troparion: "With the streams of thy tears..." Sessional hymn, Tone 3; Kontakion, Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast appeared":*

*Kontakion of Our Venerable Father Poemen:*
Thy luminous ascetical struggles, O venerable father, have today arisen as a holy memory, cheering devout souls, O Poemen divinely-wise, our venerable father!

*Our Venerable Father Moses the Black [August 28]:*[^496]
*Troparion: "A dweller of the wilderness..." Sessional hymn... Kontakion, Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast appeared":*

*Kontakion of Our Venerable Father Moses the Black:*
Having spiritually struck the faces of the demons, thou didst shine forth brightly as the sun, guiding with light our souls who honor thee, O venerable Moses!

*The Beheading of the Honorable Head of the Honorable and Glorious Prophet, Forerunner, and Baptist John [August 29]: All-Night Vigil.*[^497]
*At Small Vespers: Stichera of the Forerunner on 4; Glory, and now, of the Forerunner; on the Aposticha, of the Forerunner.*
*At Great Vespers: Blessed is the man. On "Lord, I have cried," on 8, we sing Idiomela 4 of the Forerunner, Tone 6: "In honor of the birth [of Herod]..."; and the other 3 twice; Glory, of the first tone [or Tone 6]: "In honor of the birth..."; And now, Theotokion of Tone 1.*

*..."Who doth not call thee blessed..." Entrance. Prokeimenon. Three readings for the Forerunner.*[^498]
*At the Litiya: Idiomela 3, Tone 1, and other 3, Tone 4; Glory, Tone 5: "Of an unlawful council..."; And now: "O Singer of praise..." On the Aposticha: Idiomela 3, Tone 2; and we speak the verses: "And the mouth speaking evil..."; "The righteous shall rejoice..." [Ps. 63:11]; Glory, Tone 8: "The Forerunner of the Savior..."; And now, Theotokion. Troparion, Tone 2, is said thrice: "The memory of the righteous..." [at the Polyeleos] Sessional hymn 2 and 7; blessing of loaves, and reading of the Saint.*
*At Matins: ordinary psalmody [Kathismata]. At the Polyeleos: Sessional hymns of the Forerunner. Gradual, Tone 4. Prokeimenon, Tone 4: "There will I make the horn of David to bud" [Ps. 131:17]; Verse: "Remember David, O Lord" [Ps. 131:1]. "Let every breath..." Gospel from Matthew, Tuesday of the 36th week [Matt. 14:1–13]. Sticheron of the Forerunner, with verse. Canon of the Mother of God in the Oktoechos on 6, and of the Forerunner two [canons] on 8. Katabasia: "Tracing the Cross," both choirs together. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the Forerunner; after Ode 6, Kontakion, Tone 5:*

*Kontakion of the Beheading of the Forerunner:*
The glorious beheading of the Forerunner was a certain divine dispensation, that he might preach even unto those in Hades the coming of the Savior; let Herodias weep having asked for lawless murder; for she loved not the Law of God, nor eternal life, but deceit that is temporal!

*Exapostilarion. On the Praises, of the Forerunner on 4; Glory, Tone 6: "Again Herod..."; And now, Theotokion. Great Doxology. Troparion of the Forerunner; litany, dismissal; and holy oil is given to the brethren from the lamp of the Saint. At the Liturgy: for the Forerunner, Ode 3 and Ode 6. Prokeimenon: "The righteous shall rejoice" [Ps. 63:11]; Verse: "Hear my voice, O God" [Ps. 63:2]. Epistle from the Acts [13:25–32]: "In those days, as John fulfilled his course..." Alleluia: "The righteous shall flourish like a palm tree" [Ps. 91:13]. Gospel from Mark [6:14–30]: "At that time, Herod heard of Jesus..." Communion Hymn: "In everlasting memory [shall the righteous be]" [Ps. 111:6].*[^499]
*The Holy Patriarchs of Constantinople: Alexander, John, and Paul the New [August 30]:*[^500]
*At Vespers, we do not recite psalmody [Kathisma]; Stichera: 3 of the Forerunner and 3 of the Saints; Glory, Tone 6: "Herod..."; And now, Theotokion; on the Aposticha, of the Oktoechos; Glory: "In honor of the birth..."; And now, Theotokion; Troparion of the Forerunner and of the Saints: "O God of our fathers..."*
*At Matins: Canon in the Oktoechos on 6, and of the Saints on 4, and of the Forerunner on 4. After Ode 3, Sessional hymn of the Forerunner; and after Ode 6, Kontakion of the Forerunner, Tone 4:*

*Kontakion of the Forerunner:*[^501]
Herod, having transgressed the law, O Forerunner, gave thy precious head unto a woman; venerating which, we rejoice, but Herod weeps and wails!

*On the Aposticha, of the Oktoechos; Glory, of the Forerunner; And now, Theotokion. Troparia of the Forerunner and of the Saints.*
*The Deposition of the Precious Sash of our Most Pure Lady Theotokos in Chalkoprateia [August 31]:*[^502]
*At Vespers: ordinary psalmody [Kathisma]. Stichera in the Menaion on 6; Glory, and now, Tone 2: "As a crown..."; on the Aposticha, of the Oktoechos; Glory, and now, Idiomelon, Tone 2; Troparion: "O Theotokos, Ever-Virgin..."; and Hypakoe twice. At Matins: Troparion thrice; Sessional hymn in the Oktoechos; Canon to the Mother of God in the Menaion on 6, and in the Oktoechos two [canons] on 8. After Ode 6: Kontakion, Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast appeared":*[^503]

*Kontakion of the Deposition of the Sash of the Theotokos:*
The deposition of Thy precious sash celebrates today Thy temple, O All-praised one, and diligently cries unto Thee: Rejoice, O Virgin, boast of Christians!

*On the Aposticha, of the Oktoechos. At the Liturgy: Prokeimenon, Tone 3: Song of the Mother of God: "My soul doth magnify the Lord" [Luke 1:46]; Verse: "For He hath regarded the lowliness [of His handmaiden]" [Luke 1:48]. Epistle to the Hebrews [9:1–7].*

...Brethren, the first [covenant had also ordinances of divine service, and an earthly sanctuary] [Heb. 9:1]. Alleluia: "Arise, O Lord, into Thy rest, Thou, and the ark of Thy strength" [Ps. 131:8]. Gospel from Luke [10:38–42; 11:27–28]: "At that time, Mary arose..." Communion Hymn: "I will take the cup of salvation [and call upon the name of the Lord]" [Ps. 115:4].[^504]

---

### Notes

[^198]: Folio 74v records the complete calendar commemorations for September 1 (St. Aeithalas, the 40 Holy Women and Deacon Ammoun, St. Callistus, Evodius, and Hermogenes, Joshua son of Nun, and the Great Fire of Constantinople) and sets forth the complete rubrics for Great Vespers of the Indiction and St. Symeon: Stichera at 'Lord, I have cried' (to the Indiction 3, to St. Symeon 3 Prosomoion and 4 automela), Doxasticon, Old Testament Paremias from Isaiah (61:1–9), Leviticus (26:3–12), and Wisdom (4:7–15), litanies, and the Aposticha.
[^199]: Folio 75r completes the Aposticha stichera and Psalm versicles (LXX Ps. 64:2, 5, 12) for September 1. It provides the Dismissal Troparion of the Indiction, Tone 2 ('И́же въсеꙗ твари съдѣтелю...'), the Troparion of St. Symeon the Stylite, Tone 1 ('Тръпѣнію стлъпь бысть...'), and the Dismissal Theotokion ('Ра́доуисѧ бл҃гослове́наа бц҃е дв҃о...').
[^200]: Folio 75v concludes the Dismissal Theotokion of the Indiction ('...бе бо въплъти сѧ и́ꙁбавитель мироу · є́дина є́си м҃ти · и дв҃а прѣблг҃вннаа...'). Lines 4–14 set forth the complete Matins rubrics for September 1: 'The Lord is God' in Tone 2, troparia, kathisma readings from the Life of St. Symeon, the canon order (Indiction, St. Symeon, and the 40 Holy Women) with the Katavasia of the Exaltation of the Holy Cross ('Крестъ начертавъ'), and the Kontakion of St. Symeon, Tone 2, Podoben: 'Seeking the Highest' ('Вышнихъ ищя': 'Съ вышьниими съвъкоупльшася...'). Lines 18–19 open the Praises with the incipit of the Tone 3 automelon 'Превѣчное Слово'.
[^201]: Folio 76r concludes the Liturgy of the Indiction and St. Symeon (September 1), detailing the Praises stichera, Doxology, prokeimena (LXX Ps. 146:5; LXX Ps. 115:6), Epistles (1 Tim. 2:1–7; Col. 3:12–16), Alleluia verses (LXX Ps. 64:2, 12; LXX Ps. 111:1), and Gospels (Lk. 4:16–22; Mt. 11:27–30). Lines 13–19 open September 2: the Holy Martyr Mamas and St. John the Faster, Patriarch of Constantinople, providing the Vespers stichera distribution, doxastica, and the opening incipit of the common Troparion of a Martyr, Tone 4 ('М҃чнкъ твои гн҃'). The folio bears the pencil numeral '76'.
[^202]: Folio 76v completes the Tone 4 Troparion of the Holy Martyr Mamas ('...мамонтъ · въ страдніи своє́мь вѣнець приꙗть нетлѣнныи ѿ тебе б҃а нашего...'). Lines 6–12 provide the dismissal Troparion of St. John the Faster, Tone 4 ('Правило вѣрѣ и́ ѡ́браꙁь кротости...'). Lines 12–18 provide the Matins order with the Kontakion of St. Mamas, Tone 3, Podoben: 'Today the Virgin' ('Дѣва днесь': 'Жеꙁломь ст҃е ѡ́т б҃а даннымь ти...'). Line 19 introduces the commemoration of September 3: Hieromartyr Anthimus of Nicomedia. The leaf bears the quire signature 'л҃' (quire 30).
[^203]: Folio 77r sets forth the liturgical order for September 3: Hieromartyr Anthimus of Nicomedia and Venerable Theoctistus. It gives the Vespers rubrics, the Troparion of the Hieromartyr, Tone 4 ('И́ нравомь причастникь и́ прѣтлѡмь намѣстникь а́пл҃ѡмь бывь...'), and the Troparion of the Venerable Father, Tone 8 ('Слъꙁами твоими источникь поустыни беꙁъчастное въꙁдѣлалъ є́си...'). Line 19 gives the Matins canon rubric and the incipit of the Kontakion in Tone 4, Podoben: 'Thou Who Wast Raised Up' ('Вознесыйся'). The folio bears the pencil numeral '77'.
[^204]: Folio 77v completes the Kontakion of St. Anthimus, Tone 4 ('Въ ст҃ителехъ бл҃гочт҃ивно поживъ...'), followed by the Liturgy apparatus (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 115:6, 3; Epistle Heb. 13:17–21; Alleluia LXX Ps. 131:9, LXX Ps. 111:1; Gospel Jn. 10:9–16). Lines 11–17 record the comprehensive calendar commemorations for September 4: St. Babylas of Antioch and 3 children, St. Babylas of Nicomedia and 84 children, the holy prophetesses Hermione and Eutychia (daughters of Philip the Deacon), and the holy Prophet Moses, opening their Vespers order. The leaf bears quire mark 'л҃'.
[^205]: Folio 78r concludes the September 4 service with the Troparion of the Holy Prophet Moses, Tone 2 ('Прр҃ка твое́го гн҃ па́мѧ праꙁноующе...'). It provides the Matins kontakia: to St. Babylas, Tone 8, Podoben: 'As the First-fruits' ('Яко начатки': 'Ꙗ҆ко бл҃гочт҃іа проповѣдника...'), and Tone 4, Podoben: 'Thou Hast Appeared Today' ('Явился еси': 'Веліа вѣра въ ср҃ци тво́емь...'). Lines 14–19 detail the Liturgy readings (LXX Ps. 15:3, 8; LXX Ps. 33:18; Mt. 10:32; LXX Ps. 32:1). The folio is numbered '78' in pencil.
[^206]: Folio 78v contains the full service for September 5: the Holy Prophet Zachariah, father of the Forerunner. It gives the Vespers order, the Troparion, Tone 1 ('Сщ҃енныꙗ ѡ́дежею ѡ́блечень...'), the Matins Kontakion, Tone 3, Podoben: 'Today the Virgin' ('Дѣва днесь': 'Прр҃къ дньсь іє́реи гн҃ьсъ ꙁахаріа...'), and the Liturgy apparatus (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 63:11, 2; Epistle Heb. 6:13–20; Alleluia LXX Ps. 96:11; Gospel Mt. 23:29–39). Lines 18–19 introduce the Commemoration of the Miracle of the Archangel Michael at Chonae (September 6). The leaf bears quire mark 'л҃'.
[^207]: Folio 79r contains the opening rubrics for September 6: the Commemoration of the Miracle of the Archangel Michael at Chonae (Colossae) and the Holy Martyr Eudoxius. It records the Vespers stichera, doxastica, and the Tone 4 Troparion of the Bodiless Powers ('Нб҃ныи воинствы а́рхистратиже...'). The bottom half of the leaf suffers from a severe diagonal tear (folio dissectum), resulting in the physical loss of the conclusion of the troparion and the opening Matins rubrics. The folio is numbered '79' in pencil.
[^208]: Folio 79v concludes the Liturgy of September 6 (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 103:4, 1; Epistle Heb. 2:2–10; Alleluia LXX Ps. 148:2, 5; Gospel Lk. 10:16–21; communion verse LXX Ps. 103:4). Lines 9–13 open September 7: the Forefeast of the Nativity of the Theotokos and the Holy Martyr Sozon. Owing to the diagonal tear on leaf 79, the lower half of the page is missing, truncating the Vespers stichera and dismissal troparia of the Forefeast.
[^209]: Folio 80r concludes the Forefeast of the Nativity of the Theotokos and St. Sozon (September 7), providing the Dismissal Theotokion conclusion, Troparion of the Martyr, Matins canon distribution, and the Kontakion of St. Sozon, Tone 2, Podoben: 'Seeking the Highest' ('Вышнихъ ищя': 'Ликъ честно состави мученику...'), followed by the Liturgy apparatus (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 63:11; Alleluia LXX Ps. 91:13; communion verse LXX Ps. 111:6). Lines 14–19 inaugurate the Great Feast of the Nativity of the Most Holy Theotokos (September 8), opening Small Vespers with its Tone 6 stichera and Aposticha. The leaf bears the quire signature 'л҃а' (quire 31) and pencil numeral '80'.
[^210]: Folio 80v concludes Small Vespers of the Nativity of the Theotokos (September 8), providing the Aposticha verses (LXX Ps. 44:11, 13) and the complete Dismissal Troparion, Tone 4 ('Рожство твоє бц҃е дв҃о · радость въꙁвѣсти въсеи въселенѣи · и́с тебе бо въсиꙗ слн҃це праведно́е хс҃ б҃ъ нашъ · раꙁроуши клѧтвоу · и́ дасть бл҃веніе · и́ оупраꙁнивъ съмрьть · дарова намъ животь вѣчныи :~'). Lines 9–19 set forth the Great Vespers order: 'Blessed is the man', 'Lord, I have cried' in Tone 6 on 10 (with 4 samoglasny: 'Днесь иже на разумных престолех', 'Аще и божественным', 'Днесь неплодная', and 'Нынѣ неплодная', doubling the 1st and 2nd), Entrance, prokeimenon of the day, 3 Old Testament readings, Litiya stichera ('Начало нашего спасения' in Tone 1, 'Проувиденная' in Tone 2, and Doxasticon 'В нарочитый день' in Tone 8), and opens the Aposticha stichera in Tone 4 and Doxasticon in Tone 8 ('Приидите, вси вѣрнии...').
[^211]: Folio 81r concludes the Doxasticon at the Aposticha of Great Vespers of the Nativity of the Theotokos ('...вѣрніи'), prescribes the Artoklasia troparion ('Thy Nativity, O Theotokos' 3 times), and details the complete Matins order: 'God is the Lord' with troparion 3 times, kathismata with festal sessional hymns, reading of the encomium of the feast from Simeon Metaphrastes ('въ Метафрастѣ'), Tone 4 Graduals, Prokeimenon (LXX Ps. 44:18, 2), Gospel (Lk. 1:39–49, 56), and canons: 1st canon by St. John of Damascus (Tone 2, on 8) and 2nd canon by St. Andrew of Crete (Tone 8, once), with the Katavasia of the Cross ('Крест начертав'), Hypakoe, and the complete festal Kontakion in Tone 4 ('Іѡакимь и́ а́нна поношеніа беꙁчана · а́дам же и́ евва ѡ́т ла смртныꙗ свободиста сѧ прѣчистаа стꙑмь рожьствомь твоимь · тою праꙁноують людие твои · вины грѣховныꙗ и́ꙁбавити сѧ въꙁпіюще ти неплоды ража[єть]...'). The leaf is numbered '81' in pencil.
[^212]: Folio 81v concludes the Kontakion of the Nativity of the Theotokos ('...єть бц҃ю и́ питателницю жиꙁни нашеꙗ :~'). Lines 2–8 prescribe the distribution of candles to the brethren at Ode 9, the omission of the Magnificat ('Честнѣйшую'), the Exapostilarion, Praises stichera in Tone 1 on 4 ('О преславное чудо'), Doxasticon in Tone 6 ('Сей день Господень'), Great Doxology, anointing with holy oil, and the First Hour. Lines 9–16 detail the Divine Liturgy order (canon odes 3 and 6, troparion and kontakion at the Little Entrance, prokeimenon Lk. 1:46, 48, Epistle Phil. 2:5–11, Alleluia LXX Ps. 44:11, 13, Gospel Lk. 10:38–42; 11:27–28), noting the 5-day Afterfeast until the apodosis on September 12. Lines 16–19 open the Saturday before the Exaltation (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 46:7, 2).
[^213]: Folio 82r sets forth the liturgical apparatus for Saturday and Sunday before the Exaltation: Saturday Epistle (1 Cor. 2:6–9) and Gospel (Jn. 14:21–24); Sunday before the Exaltation Liturgy (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 27:9, 1; Epistle Gal. 6:11–18; Alleluia LXX Ps. 29:2; Gospel Jn. 3:13–17). Lines 7–19 open September 9: the Holy and Righteous Ancestors of God Joachim and Anna, and the Holy Martyr Severian, providing Vespers stichera, Doxasticon in Tone 5 ('И блаженная двоице'), Matins rubrics, and the opening of the Kontakion of SS. Joachim and Anna in Tone 2 ('Веселит сѧ нн҃ѣ а́нна · неплоства раꙁрѣшьшине ꙗ́съоуꙁъ · и́ питаеть прѣчистоую · съꙁывающи к вѣкь въспѣвати даровавшаго ·'). The leaf bears quire mark 'л҃а' (quire 31) and pencil numeral '82'.
[^214]: Folio 82v concludes the Kontakion of SS. Joachim and Anna, Tone 2 ('...ѡ́ ложеснь є́ꙗ ꙁемнымь · є́диноу мт҃рь моужененскоуисна :~'). Lines 2–9 give the Liturgy apparatus for September 9 (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 67:36, 27; Epistle Heb. 9:1–7; Alleluia LXX Ps. 36:39; Gospel Lk. 8:16–21; communion verse LXX Ps. 32:1). Lines 10–19 set forth the commemorations for September 10: the Holy Virgin-Martyrs Menodora, Metrodora, and Nymphodora, giving Vespers stichera, Matins canon distribution (feast on 6, martyrs on 4), and Liturgy apparatus (prokeimenon LXX Ps. 67:36; Epistle 2 Cor. 6:1–10; Alleluia LXX Ps. 39:2).
[^215]: Folio 83r concludes the September 10 Liturgy (Gospel from Luke and communion verse). Lines 2–14 set forth the commemorations for September 11 (Venerable Theodora of Alexandria) combined with September 12 (Hieromartyr Autonomos), detailing the combined canon distribution (feast on 6, St. Theodora on 4, St. Autonomos on 4) and the complete Kontakion of St. Autonomos in Tone 8 ('Ꙗко свѣтила прп҃бна · и́ страдалца вѣрна · цр҃кви твоущне славныдь · автономе свѣтилниче всемирныи · и тр҃ци оугодниче · моли чл҃колюбца :~'). Lines 15–18 open the Apodosis of the Nativity of the Theotokos (September 12) with Vespers stichera on 6, samoglasny of Tone 6, prokeimenon of the day without entrance or readings, and reading from St. John Chrysostom. The folio is numbered '83' in pencil.
[^216]: Folio 83v completes the Apodosis of the Nativity of the Theotokos with the Matins order: two canons (on 8 and on 6), Katavasia of the Cross ('Крест начертав'), festal sessional hymn, kontakion, omission of 'More honorable', Praises stichera on 3 ('О дивное чудо'), Doxasticon in Tone 6 ('Сей день Господень'), Great Doxology, and Liturgy readings. Lines 11–19 open September 13: the Dedication (Encaenia) of the Church of the Holy Resurrection in Jerusalem, Hieromartyr Cornelius the Centurion, and Forefeast of the Exaltation of the Cross, giving Vespers samoglasny, Doxasticon in Tone 6 ('Память обновления'), Cross doxasticon ('Днесь древо'), 3 Dedication readings, and Aposticha stichera of the Cross in Tone 5 ('Радуйся, живоносный Кресте').
[^217]: Folio 84r concludes the Aposticha of September 13 with the Dedication Doxasticon (Tone 2) and Cross Doxasticon ('Божественное сокровище'). Lines 2–12 record the full text of two dismissal troparia: the Troparion of the Dedication, Tone 4 ('Ꙗ́ко вышнꙗꙗ твръдость и бл҃голѣпіе · и нижнꙗꙗ ꙗ́виль є́си красотоу · ст҃го селеньꙗ славы тво́еꙗ гн҃ · оутверди є́го въ вѣкы и вѣка · и приими наша · ꙗ́же въ не́мь непрестанно приносимыꙗ ти мл҃твы · бц҃а ради всѣхь животе и въскрсеніе :~'), and the Troparion of the Cross, Tone 2 ('Животворащии кр҃тъ тво́еꙗ бл҃гости · є́гоже дарова намь · недостоиныимь гн҃ тебѣ приносимь на мл҃твꙋ · сп҃си цр҃ꙗ · и́ гра сво́и · молаща сѧ бц҃а ради є́дине чл҃колюбче :~'). Lines 13–19 set forth the Matins order: troparia after the kathismata, three canons (Dedication on 4, Cross on 4, St. Cornelius on 4), omission of the Magnificat, and the Kontakion of the Dedication in Tone 2 ('Ѡ́бновленіе ср҃дца дх҃ѡ · и просвѣщенье оутробы ѡ́бнови · вѣрою съвр[шающихъ]...'). The folio is numbered '84' in pencil.
[^218]: Folio 84v concludes the Tone 2 Kontakion of the Dedication ('...поновленіе до́моу тво́е́го храма є́гоже бл҃говоли съꙁдати сѧ · въ твоє́ бжест҃вьноє и́мѧ · є́дине въ ст҃ы почиваꙗи :~'). Lines 4–7 give the second Kontakion of the Dedication, Tone 4, Podoben: 'Thou Hast Appeared Today' ('По̑ ꙗ́ви N бо свѣтлое и́ вне цр҃кви въсѣмь · просвѣщающа вѣрьныꙗ въ ненже стоꙗще въꙁпіемь · сіи храмь оутверди гн҃ :~'), where the printer sets the curious typographical variant 'По̑ яви N бо' for 'Небо светлое'. Lines 7–10 provide the Praises stichera (Tone 4 on 4, doubling the 1st), Tone 3 Doxasticon ('К себе возвращаяся, человече'), and Great Doxology. Lines 10–19 detail the Liturgy readings: prokeimenon LXX Ps. 92:5, 1; Epistle Heb. 2:11–18; Alleluia LXX Ps. 86:1–3; Gospel Mt. 16:13–19; communion verse LXX Ps. 25:8.
[^219]: Folio 85r contains two major liturgical monuments: Lines 1–7 record the ritual procession of bringing forth the Precious Cross from the sacristy (сосудохранительница / skevophylakion) by the ecclesiarch, priest, and censer-bearer (kandilaptēs) after Small Vespers, transferring it to the Holy Table while singing 'Save, O Lord, Thy people' and lighting a candle for the entire night. Lines 8–19 open the Universal Exaltation of the Precious and Life-Giving Cross (September 14), accompanied by a crucial historical-rubrical note on the collision with the Dormition of St. John Chrysostom, explaining why the office of St. John Chrysostom is transferred to November 13 in the Constantinopolitan (Studite) tradition, while preserved in the Jerusalem (Sabaitic) Typikon. The leaf is numbered '85' in pencil.
[^220]: Folio 91v opens September 20: the Holy Great-Martyr Eustathius (Placidus), his wife Theopiste, and their children Agapius and Theopistus. Crucially, a significant physical lacuna occurs in the surviving Krakow exemplar between Folio 85r (Page 170 in Cohort 17, concluding the rubrics of the Exaltation on September 14) and Folio 91v (Page 171), spanning September 14 to 19 (including the Apodosis of the Exaltation and the commemorations of SS. Nicetas, Euphemia, Sophia and her daughters, and the Forefeast of Eustathius). Lines 1–8 set forth the Vespers and Matins order: stichera at "Lord, I have cried" (3 of the feast, 3 of the martyr; Doxasticon in Tone 2, 'Второе небо'; Both now, festal), Aposticha (Doxasticon in Tone 6, 'Адаманта душевную'), the complete Dismissal Troparion of the Martyrs, Tone 4 ('Мч҃ници твои гн҃ · въ страданіихъ своихъ вѣнца приꙗша нетлѣнныꙗ ѡ́ тебе б҃а нашего...'), Matins kathismata, canons (feast on 6, martyrs on 6), Kontakion of the feast after Ode 3, and lines 9–14 provide the full text of the Kontakion of St. Eustathius in Tone 2 ('В вышнихъ ища · стр҃ти хв҃ѣ · ꙗквѣ оуподобилесѧ єси и того вѣрно испивь чашю · ѡ́бещникь єѵстафье · и славѣ є́го причастникь бы · ѡ́ самого х҃а б҃а въсѣхь приє́мла є́сь вышє бж҃твьное ѡ́ставление :~'). Lines 14–19 detail the Divine Liturgy readings: prokeimenon LXX Ps. 15:3, 8; Epistle to the Ephesians [Eph. 6:10–17, pericope 233]; Alleluia LXX Ps. 33:18; Gospel from Luke [Lk. 21:12–19, pericope 106]; and communion verse LXX Ps. 32:1.
[^221]: Folio 92r (marked '92' in pencil at the top right) preserves two liturgical units: Lines 1–3 contain the fragmentary conclusion of the sticheron or kontakion of Hieromartyr Phocas of Sinope (September 22), recording the rare colometric witness: '...ѡ́ а҃ггл҃а оу҆крѣплꙗ́ємь · н см҃ртыиꙁволаꙗ съ въпиющи дити приди фоко сладн · инъ никтоже наны :~'. Lines 3–10 give the complete Kontakion of the Holy Apostle Quadratus (Кодрат), Tone 8, Podoben: "As First-Fruits" ('И́ страстотр҃пца крѣпка вселенаа приносить ти гн҃ · бж҃твнаго кондрата ап҃ла · пѣ́ьми въспѣвающе чт҃ноу ю́ є́го памѧть · просѧще въсегда прегрѣшеніе́мь ѡ́ставленьꙗ · є́го ради даровати поющнди тꙗ чл҃овѣколюбче :~'), with Liturgy readings and communion verse LXX Ps. 111:6. Lines 11–19 open September 23: the Conception of the Honorable, Glorious Prophet, Forerunner, and Baptist John (Зачатье іѡ́анна пр҃тчи), marking it as a great feast ('праꙁноуєве днь') with marginal numeral 'к҃г', giving Vespers stichera on 6, Tone 6 Doxastica ('О неплодныя' and 'Ангел вопияше'), and the complete Dismissal Troparion, Tone 4 ('Прежене ражающіа неплоды , въꙁвеселисѧ · се бо зачала́ є́си · сл҃нцю свѣтилника ꙗ́вѣ · просвѣтити хотѧщаго всю вселеноую · ѡ́мраченіемь болѧщю · ликоуи ꙁахарьꙗ въпиꙗ съ дръꙁновеніемь · пр҃ркъ вышняго є́ хотѧи родити :~').
[^222]: Folio 92v completes the feast of the Conception of St. John the Baptist and opens September 24: Lines 1–7 record the Matins canons (Octoechos and Forerunner on 6) and the complete Kontakion of the Conception in Tone 1, Podoben: "In the Womb" ('Веселит сѧ свѣтло ꙁахаріꙗ великыи · съ прѣхвалною єлисафеѳою съкръстницею [сопряжницею] · достоино ꙁаченьшні іѡ́анна пр҃тчю · є́гоже а҃ггл҃ь бл҃говѣсти радостно · и́ чл҃ци досто длъжно почитают оу҆чителꙗ бл҃гти :~').
